- **rlens-readability**: 변경된 코드의 호출부/시그니처 인간공학만 보는 review-lens. API가 호출자 입장에서 명확하고 오용하기 어려운가. review-oracle-first에서 호출. ([pdev-jay/cidd-cc](https://github.com/pdev-jay/cidd-cc/blob/HEAD/agents/rlens-readability.md))
- **rlens-security-logic**: SAST/타입이 못 잡는 로직 수준 보안 문제만 보는 review-lens. auth/입력/권한을 건드리는 변경에만 켠다(task-gated). review-oracle-first 스킬에서 호출. ([pdev-jay/cidd-cc](https://github.com/pdev-jay/cidd-cc/blob/HEAD/agents/rlens-security-logic.md))
- **rlens-simplicity**: 변경된 코드가 *필요 이상으로* 길고 복잡한지 — 줄일 수 있는데 안 줄인 우발적 복잡도(accidental complexity)만 보는 review-lens. 리팩토링/과복잡 리뷰. 줄 수·순환복잡도·중복 카운트는 메트릭(오라클) 몫이고, lens는 "본질이냐 우발이냐 + 더 단순한 형태"를 판정. review-oracle-first 스킬에서 호출. ([pdev-jay/cidd-cc](https://github.com/pdev-jay/cidd-cc/blob/HEAD/agents/rlens-simplicity.md))

- **Robin – Execution Architect**: Call when: translating architectural decisions into module structure, designing internal interfaces between Python modules, reviewing Workorders for architectural alignment, creating ADRs for execution-level decisions, defining import boundaries, or assessing structural impact of a planned change. ([boehmert/agentic-coding-project-template](https://github.com/boehmert/agentic-coding-project-template/blob/HEAD/.agent-migration/archive-20260616/agents/architect.agent.md))
- **robin-committer**: AI-Robin Committer Agent. Executes a git commit using the exact message provided by the trigger signal. Invoked only by the AI-Robin kernel after review_merged or degradation_spec_written. ([waynewangyuxuan/Robin](https://github.com/waynewangyuxuan/Robin/blob/HEAD/agents/robin-committer.md))
- **robin-degrader**: AI-Robin Degrader Agent. Writes the context-degraded-*.yaml spec with narrative and updates escalation-notice.md. Invoked only by the AI-Robin kernel when a scope is degraded. ([waynewangyuxuan/Robin](https://github.com/waynewangyuxuan/Robin/blob/HEAD/agents/robin-degrader.md))
- **robin-executor**: AI-Robin Executor Agent. Writes actual application code for one milestone. May modify source files and run tests. Does NOT git commit (kernel does that). Invoked only by the AI-Robin kernel. ([waynewangyuxuan/Robin](https://github.com/waynewangyuxuan/Robin/blob/HEAD/agents/robin-executor.md))
- **robin-finalizer**: AI-Robin Finalizer Agent. Generates the end-of-run delivery bundle (DELIVERY.md). Invoked only by the AI-Robin kernel on all_complete. ([waynewangyuxuan/Robin](https://github.com/waynewangyuxuan/Robin/blob/HEAD/agents/robin-finalizer.md))
- **robin-intake**: AI-Robin Intake Agent. Intake stage — the only sub-agent that interacts with the user. Do not invoke for general intake tasks; only invoke as part of an AI-Robin dispatch loop. ([waynewangyuxuan/Robin](https://github.com/waynewangyuxuan/Robin/blob/HEAD/agents/robin-intake.md))
- **robin-merger**: AI-Robin Merger Agent. Consolidates N review sub-verdicts into one merged verdict with a composed git commit message. Invoked only by the AI-Robin kernel after all review playbooks return. ([waynewangyuxuan/Robin](https://github.com/waynewangyuxuan/Robin/blob/HEAD/agents/robin-merger.md))
- **robin-planner**: AI-Robin Planner Agent. Turns Intake's intake into an executable plan with milestones, modules, and contract specs. Invoked only by the AI-Robin kernel. ([waynewangyuxuan/Robin](https://github.com/waynewangyuxuan/Robin/blob/HEAD/agents/robin-planner.md))
- **robin-researcher**: AI-Robin Researcher Agent. Answers a specific factual question via web search. Invoked only by the AI-Robin kernel when Planning returns planning_needs_research. ([waynewangyuxuan/Robin](https://github.com/waynewangyuxuan/Robin/blob/HEAD/agents/robin-researcher.md))
- **robin-review-planner**: AI-Robin Review Planner Agent. Meta-agent that decides which review playbooks to run for a batch. Invoked only by the AI-Robin kernel at the start of every review stage. ([waynewangyuxuan/Robin](https://github.com/waynewangyuxuan/Robin/blob/HEAD/agents/robin-review-planner.md))
- **robin-reviewer**: Persona for mugiwara-review. Doubt-driven diff reviewer, breaking-change map, reliability rating. Read-only. ([ionivetech/mugiwara](https://github.com/ionivetech/mugiwara/blob/HEAD/content/agents/robin-reviewer.md))
- **robin-reviewer-code-quality**: AI-Robin code-quality reviewer. - **roblox-reviewer**: Reviews Roblox game changes for the two failures that ruin games - a server that trusts the client, and player data that can be lost. Use before committing anything that touches remotes, currency, progress, purchases or DataStore. ([szlay/roblox-base](https://github.com/szlay/roblox-base/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/roblox-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **roblox-reviewer**: Read-only auditor for Roblox/Luau code. Reviews remote security, DataStore and purchase safety, memory/performance, strict-typing quality and API currency (deprecated APIs, new type solver), and returns severity-sorted findings with concrete failure scenarios. Use proactively after writing or modifying Roblox gameplay systems, and for roblox-review audits. ([JashanMaan28/roblox-agent-kit](https://github.com/JashanMaan28/roblox-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/roblox-reviewer.md))
- **roblox-scout**: Locates code in a Roblox project and returns a table of paths and lines instead of file contents. Use to find where something is defined, what calls it, every RemoteEvent or RemoteFunction handler, every DataStore or ProfileStore call, or the entry points of a system, when the search would take more than three reads or searches in the main conversation. Read-only, and it judges nothing. Skip it when the file is already named, or when the project lives only in Studio with no source files on disk. ([andrian-syh/roblox-optimum](https://github.com/andrian-syh/roblox-optimum/blob/HEAD/agents/roblox-scout.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **roblox-security-reviewer**: Audits Roblox/Luau code for exploit surface — unvalidated remote arguments, client-trusted damage or currency, missing rate limits, secrets in replicated containers, dupe-prone read-modify-write, and data-loss risks in DataStore code. Use on any diff that crosses the client/server boundary or touches player data. ([waleeds-mansour/Claude-Plugin](https://github.com/waleeds-mansour/Claude-Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/roblox-security-reviewer.md))
- **roblox-specialist**: Expert Roblox (2025+) and Luau specialist with strict typing.
